# Phase 2 Report: Title Lineage, Sovereign Trajectories & Historical Grounding

* **Campaign Date:** 18 June 1171 (`chatenois_867_01_01.ck3`)
* **Audit Scope:** **2,644 Counties** in the Roman Empire (`h_roman_empire`) across **183 De Jure Kingdoms**
* **Newly Integrated Frontier:** **903 Expansion Counties** (1154 $\rightarrow$ 1171) across **93 Kingdoms**
* **Analysis Pipeline:** Historical Sovereign Trajectory → Colonizer Presence Audit → Cultural Footprint Mapping → 3-Tier Classification

---

## 1. Executive Summary & 3-Tier Classification Breakdown

Phase 2 maps the sovereign lineage and cultural evolution of every county from the **867 AD alternate-history divergence point** to the **1171 AD save state**, dismantling the static vanilla CK3 map bias and the OTL calendar gap under the **Three-Tier Classification System**.

| Classification Tier | Imperial Counties | Percentage | Evaluation Rule & Action |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **Tier 1: Pre-867 Historical / Antiquity** | **208** | 7.9% | **VALID**: Attested pre-867 Roman, Celtic, Greek, Slavic, or Germanic centre; trace chronological culture changes and adapt phonologically to Tarraconian. |
| **Tier 2: Founded After 867 on Hydronymic, Geographic, or Tribal Stems** | **68** | 2.6% | **PRE-HRE MORPHOLOGY RESTORATION**: Settlement founded after 867 on ancient river/mountain/tribe (e.g. *Minsk*, *Volkovysk*, *Memel*, *Bartenstein*); restore pre-HRE native morphology (strip colonial -sk/-burg/-stein) before Tarraconian adaptation. |
| **Tier 3: Other Settlements Founded After 867 (Default)** | **2368** | 89.6% | **ALTERNATIVE SELECTION (FLAGGED)**: Dynastic castles (*Burg Berge*), personal foundations (*Lviv*), descriptive outposts (*Novogrudok*), or unverified titles; pick another name via Options A/B/C, changing appropriately to linguistic history. |
| **TOTAL IMPERIAL COUNTIES AUDITED** | **2644** | **100.0%** | **2436 Total Flagged Titles (92.1%)** requiring historical pre-867 resolution or morphology audit in Phase 4. |

---

## 2. Key Findings: The Colonizer Presence Audit

A critical divergence between real-world history and the campaign timeline is that **historical colonial empires never expanded into regions they historically transformed**:

1. **The Baltic & Prussia (Zero Teutonic Order Continuity):**
   * In real history, the Northern Crusades and Teutonic Order founded fortress-colonies in the 13th century (*Marienburg*, *Königsberg*, *Allenstein*, *Bartenstein*, *Memel*).
   * In the campaign save, the **Teutonic Order never held a single Baltic county**. Prussia, Curonia, and Samogitia progressed directly from indigenous Old Prussian and Baltic pagan rule into the **Tarraconian Roman Empire** via Imperial appointments starting in the 1040s–1080s.
   * *Result:* German colonial suffixes (`-burg`, `-stein`) are stripped in Tier 2 to restore pre-HRE Baltic stems (*Mēmele* → *Memel*, *Barta* → *Barten*), while Tier 3 castle foundations revert to ancient pre-867 centres (`b_truso` for *Marienburg*, `b_twangste` for *Königsberg*).

2. **Finland & Karelia (Zero Swedish Crusade Footprint):**
   * In real history, Sweden colonized the southern coast of Finland (*Nyland* = New Land, *Borgå*, *Pedersöre*, *Viborg*) during the Swedish Crusades (1150–1293).
   * In the campaign save, **Sweden never ruled Finland**. The Finnish tribes maintained native sovereignty until direct integration into the Empire under Augustus Wernhard.
   * *Result:* Swedish colonial names (*Nyland*, *Pedersöre*) are resolved via native Finnic roots (*Uusimaa*, *Pietarsaari*, *Porvoo*).

3. **Pontic Steppe & Volga Basin (Zero Cossack / Tsarist Imperial Footprint):**
   * Vanilla CK3 map keys in the Pontic steppe contain Early Modern Tsarist Russian fortress and Cossack settlements (*Bakhmut* 1571, *Millerovo* 1786, *Salavat*, *Chelyabinsk*).
   * In the save, the steppe transitioned directly from **Pecheneg** and **Cuman / Kipchak** nomadic control into the Roman Empire.
   * *Result:* Resolved via ancient hydronyms (*Tanais / Don*, *Borysthenes / Dnêper*, *Bakhmutka*).

4. **Outremer & Egypt (Zero First Crusade Continuity):**
   * Real-world crusader fortresses (*Montréal / Shawbak* built 1115 by Baldwin I) and Fatimid foundations (*Cairo / al-Qāhira* founded 969) have no continuity with the save's direct Abbasid → Roman Imperial trajectory.
   * *Result:* Replaced by Classical Roman/Hellenistic centres (*Petra / Rekem* for Montréal, *Fustat / Babylon in Aegypto* for Cairo).

## 3. Sovereign Trajectories & Historical Grounding of Newly Integrated Frontier Titles (1154 $\rightarrow$ 1171)

Between 1154 and 1171, the Roman Empire under Pope Gotzelo expanded across **903 newly integrated counties** in **93 de jure kingdoms**.

### 3.1 Frontier 3-Tier Classification Breakdown

| Frontier Classification Tier | Counties | Percentage of Frontier | Status |
| :--- | :---: | :---: | :--- |
| **Tier 1: Pre-867 Antiquity / Classical Provincial Centers** | **14** | 1.6% | Valid ancient centres (Indic mahajanapadas, Sri Lankan ratas, Dvaravati Mon cities, Pyu capitals, Tang/Ritsuryo sites) |
| **Tier 2: Founded After 867 on Hydronymic/Geographic Stems** | **14** | 1.6% | Pre-HRE morphology restorations on ancient watercourses/mountains/tribal stems |
| **Tier 3: Other Post-867 Foundations / Unverified (Default)** | **875** | 96.9% | Flagged for Option A (Sub-Barony), B (Hydronym), or C (Founder) replacement |
| **TOTAL NEWLY INTEGRATED TITLES** | **903** | **100.0%** | **889 Flagged Frontier Titles (98.4%)** |

### 3.2 Regional Frontier Clusters & Sovereign Grounding (1154 $\rightarrow$ 1171)

* **South Asia — Bengal, Kamarupa, Bihar, Deccan & Lanka (59 counties):** Ancient Indian mahajanapadas and classical South Indian capitals (`k_bengal`, `k_bihar`, `k_orissa`, `k_kamarupa`, `k_karnata`, `k_tamilakam`, `k_telingana`, `k_andhra`, `k_lanka`, `k_sindh`). Post-867 foundations (`c_vijayapura`, `c_laksmanavati`, `c_charaideo`, `c_goalpara`, `c_kamatapur`, `c_racakonda`, `c_candragiri`, `c_muluvagil`, `c_penugonda`, `c_batticaloa`, `c_midnapore`) resolved to pre-867 classical seats.
* **Mainland Southeast Asia — Burma, Dvaravati & Chenla (56 counties):** Ancient Pyu and Mon centres (`c_pagan`, `c_sriksetra`, `c_hanlan`, `c_thaton`, `c_pegu`, `c_nakhon_pathom`, `c_lop_buri`, `c_haripunjaya`, `c_canasha`). Post-867 capitals (`c_ava`, `c_toungoo`, `c_yamethin`, `c_sagaing`, `c_nyaungshwe`, `c_sukhothai`, `c_phetchabun`, `c_mukdahan`, `c_thanbyuzayat`) resolved via Options A/B.
* **Maritime Southeast Asia & Taiwan (118 counties):** Ancient entrepôts (`k_malayadvipa`, `k_yavakadvipa`, `k_tanjungnagara`, `k_hujung_medini`, `k_lusung`, `k_kabisay-an`, `k_sulawesi`, `k_maluku`, `k_liuqiu`); modern/colonial and late sultanate ports (`c_MAL_johor`, `c_SUM_east_sumatra`, `c_SUM_pekanbaru`, `c_SUM_dharmasraya`, `c_BOR_kudat`, `c_BOR_lahad_datu`, `c_BOR_martapura`, `c_IDO_barat_daya`) resolved to pre-867 seats.
* **Amur, Hokkaido, Tibet & Inner Asian Corridors (121 counties):** Calqued Sinosphere borderlands (`k_amur`, `k_aynumosir`, `k_luzhen`, `k_balhae`, `k_raole`), Tibetan marches (`k_maryul`, `k_lhomon`, `k_tsang`), and Steppe/Silk Road corridors (`k_oghuz_il`, `k_cuman`, `k_caspian_steppe`, `k_zhetysu`, `k_dzungaria`, `k_gobi`, `k_otuken`, `k_syr_darya`, `k_transoxiana`, `k_khotan`, `k_qara_dala`, `k_kabulistan`, `k_khorasan`).
* **East Africa, Sudan, Balkans & Pontic/Bashkir Steppes (58 counties):** Swahili/East African coast (`k_zanj`, `k_bale`, `k_adal`, `k_darfur`, `k_nubia`, `k_africa`) plus `k_moldavia`, `k_pontic_steppe`, `k_bashkiria`, `x_script_6717`, and `k_georgia`.

## 5. Toponym-First Realignment of Cadet Houses & Dynamic Emergent Cultures

Under the **Toponym-First Principle**, landed titles represent physical geography, antiquity urban centres, and Roman territorial divisions. Anachronistic titles cannot be justified or grandfathered by in-game cadet branches or dynamic emergent cultures. Instead, causality is strictly reversed: **noble houses and dynamic cultures derive their names from the historically resolved titles**.

### 5.1 Re-evaluation of Previous 'Canon' Titles

Titles previously given false exemptions are now classified strictly under the 3-Tier model:
* **Validated Antiquity Centres (Tier 1):** Titles like `c_metz` (*Divodurum*), `c_buthrotum` (*Buthrotum*), `c_lecce` (*Lupiae*), `c_ragusa` (*Ragusium*), `c_theodosiopolis`, `c_dvin`, `c_isfahan`, `c_hamadan`, `c_carcassonne`, and `c_uses` are validated as **Tier 1 (Antiquity)** on their own historical merits.
* **Post-867 Hydronymic & Tribal Titles (Tier 2):** Titles like `c_minsk` (*Mėna*), `c_volkovysk` (*Ros'*), `c_memel` (*Mēmele*), `c_bartenstein` (*Barta*), and `c_metzovo` are audited for pre-HRE morphology and adapted to Tarraconian.
* **Unmasked Post-867 Settlements (Tier 3 Default):** Titles like `c_novogrudok` (New Castle 1044), `c_bergh` (Burg Berge 1077), `c_luxembourg` (963), `c_bouillon` (988), `c_namur` (907), `c_zagreb` (1094), `c_aalborg` (1040), `c_lviv` (1256), `c_galati` (1445), `c_bugeac` (16th c.), `c_dorohoi` (1408), `c_cetatea_alba` (1350), `c_chilia` (1318), `c_montpellier` (985), and `c_tinmallal` (1124) are unmasked and flagged as **Tier 3** for resolution via Options A/B/C.

## 7. Next Steps & Transition to Phase 3

1. **Phase 3 (Automated Screening & Reconciliation Matrix):**
   * Screen all flagged Tier 2 & Tier 3 titles against the compiled dictionary of ancient toponyms, hydronyms, and pre-HRE morphologies.
   * Finalize the sub-barony candidate mapping for every flagged county.
2. **Phase 4 (Resolution Pipeline & Cadet Branch Renames):**
   * For Tier 2 titles: Restore pre-HRE native morphology (e.g., Yatvingian/Baltic *Mėna* → *Mêne*, *Rūsa* → *Rôse*, *Mēmele* → *Memel*, *Barta* → *Barten*).
   * For Tier 3 titles: Finalize Option A (Sub-Barony), Option B (Hydronym), or Option C (Founder Personality) resolutions.
   * Apply Tarraconian sound laws to generate official Chancellery names and adjectives.
   * Systematically update cadet branch toponymic suffixes (`Kestenholz-<Seat>`).
